使用 AI 模型理解和响应编码请求\n- **多模型支持** - 支持 Anthropic Claude 和 OpenAI 模型\n- **代码编辑** - 通过智能建议直接编辑文件\n- **代码库理解** - 分析项目结构和代码关系\n- **命令执行** - 运行 shell 命令并获得实时反馈\n- **工具系统** - 可扩展架构，支持专用工具\n- **Agent Skills** - 通过 SKILL.md 按需扩展能力\n- **子代理系统** - 将任务委托给具有自己工具范围的专用代理\n- **丰富 UI** - 美观的终端界面，支持语法高亮\n- **文件操作** - 读取、写入、编辑、搜索和管理文件\n- **任务跟踪** - 为每个项目规划、读取和更新持久化任务列表\n- **后台命令** - 在后台运行命令并监控输出\n- **权限系统** - 安全模式，操作需要权限确认\n- **批量编辑支持** - 对文件进行批量编辑操作\n- **MCP 服务器支持** - 与 Model Context Protocol 服务器集成\n- **会话管理** - 持久化会话历史和用量跟踪\n- **Jupyter Notebook 支持** - 直接编辑 .ipynb 文件\n- **Hooks 系统** - 在生命周期事件执行自定义脚本，支持决策控制\n- **自定义命令** - 定义可复用的斜杠命令，支持参数替换\n\n## 安装\n\n### 快速安装\n从 git 仓库安装：\n```bash\npip install git+https://github.com/quantmew/ripperdoc.git\n```\n\n或从源码安装：\n```bash\n# 克隆仓库\ngit clone https://github.com/quantmew/ripperdoc.git\ncd ripperdoc\n\n# 从源码安装\npip install -e .\n```\n\n## 使用\n\n### 交互模式（推荐）\n```bash\nripperdoc\n# 或使用短别名\nrd\n```\n\n这将启动一个交互式会话，您可以：\n- 询问关于代码库的问题\n- 请求代码修改\n- 执行命令\n- 导航和探索文件\n\n**选项：**\n- `--yolo` - 跳过权限提示（默认启用安全模式）\n- `--model <模型名>` - 指定模型（如 `claude-sonnet-4-20250514`、`gpt-4o`）\n- `--tools <工具列表>` - 过滤可用工具（逗号分隔，或 "" 表示禁用）\n- `--no-mcp` - 禁用 MCP 服务器集成\n- `--verbose` - 启用详细日志\n\n### 快速开始\n\n如需引导式介绍，请查看 [QUICKSTART.md](QUICKSTART.md) 指南。\n\n### Python SDK（无头模式）\n\n通过 Python SDK 使用 Ripperdoc，无需终端 UI。查看 [SDK 文档](https://ripperdoc-docs.pages.dev/docs/sdk-overview/) 了解一次性 `query` 助手和基于会话的 `RipperdocClient` 的示例。\n\n#### SDK 示例\n\n- **基础用法**：简单的一次性查询\n- **会话管理**：具有上下文的持久化会话\n- **工具集成**：直接工具访问和自定义\n- **配置**：自定义模型提供商和设置\n\n查看 [examples/](examples/) 目录获取完整的 SDK 使用示例。\n\n### 安全模式权限\n\n安全模式默认启用。收到提示时：\n- 输入 `y` 或 `yes` 允许单个操作\n- 输入 `a` 或 `always` 允许会话期间所有该类操作\n- 输入 `n` 或 `no` 拒绝操作\n\n使用 `--yolo` 标志跳过所有权限提示：\n```bash\nripperdoc --yolo\n```\n\n### Agent Skills\n\n用可复用的 Skill 包扩展 Ripperdoc：\n\n- **个人技能**：`~/.ripperdoc/skills/<技能名>/SKILL.md`\n- **项目技能**：`.ripperdoc/skills/<技能名>/SKILL.md`（可提交到 git）\n- 每个 `SKILL.md` 以 YAML 头开始：\n  - `name` - 技能标识符\n  - `description` - 技能功能描述\n  - `allowed-tools`（可选）- 限制技能可使用的工具\n  - `model`（可选）- 为该技能建议特定模型\n  - `max-thinking-tokens`（可选）- 控制思考预算\n  - `disable-model-invocation`（可选）- 不调用模型直接使用技能\n- 相关文件可放在 `SKILL.md` 同目录\n- 技能自动发现并通过 `Skill` 工具按需加载\n\n**内置技能**：PDF 处理（`pdf`）、PowerPoint（`pptx`）、Excel（`xlsx`）\n\n## 示例\n\n### 代码分析\n```\n> 你能解释这个函数的作用吗？\n> 查找所有引用 `parse_config` 函数的地方\n```\n\n### 文件操作\n```\n> 读取 main.py 文件并建议改进\n> 创建一个名为 UserProfile.tsx 的新组件\n> 更新所有导入以使用新的包结构\n```\n\n### 代码生成\n```\n> 创建一个实现 REST API 客户端的新 Python 脚本\n> 为 auth 模块生成单元测试\n> 为数据库连接代码添加错误处理\n```\n\n### 项目导航\n```\n> 显示项目中所有的 Python 文件\n> 找到用户认证逻辑的实现位置\n> 列出项目中所有 API 端点\n```\n\n### MCP 集成\n```\n> 有哪些可用的 MCP 服务器？\n> 查询 context7 文档中关于 React hooks 的内容\n```\n\n## 开发\n\n### 设置开发环境\n```bash\n# 安装开发依赖\npip install -e ".[dev]"\n\n# 运行测试\npytest\n\n# 类型检查\nmypy ripperdoc\n\n# 代码格式化\nblack ripperdoc\n\n# 代码检查\nruff ripperdoc\n```\n\n\n\n## 许可证\n\n本项目采用 Apache 2.0 许可证 - Console()\nlogger = get_logger()\n\n\ndef parse_tools_option(tools_arg: Optional[str]) -> Optional[List[str]]:\n    """Parse the --tools argument.\n\n    Args:\n        tools_arg: The raw tools argument from CLI.\n\n    Returns:\n        None for default (all tools), empty list for "" (no tools),\n        or a list of tool names for filtering.\n    """\n    if tools_arg is None:\n        return None  # Use all default tools\n\n    tools_arg = tools_arg.strip()\n\n    if tools_arg == "":\n        return []  # Disable all tools\n\n    if tools_arg.lower() == "default":\n        return None  # Use all default tools\n\n    # Parse comma-separated list\n    tool_names = [name.strip() for name in tools_arg.split(",") if name.strip()]\n\n    # Validate tool names\n    invalid_tools = [name for name in tool_names if name not in BUILTIN_TOOL_NAMES]\n    if invalid_tools:\n        logger.warning(\n            "[cli] Unknown tools specified: %s. Available tools: %s",\n            ", ".join(invalid_tools),\n            ", ".join(BUILTIN_TOOL_NAMES),\n        )\n\n    return tool_names if tool_names else None\n\n\ndef parse_csv_option(raw_value: Optional[str]) -> Optional[List[str]]:\n    """Parse a comma-separated CLI option into a list."""\n    if raw_value is None:\n        return None\n    value = raw_value.strip()\n    if value == "":\n        return []\n    return [item.strip() for item in value.split(",") if item.strip()]\n\n\nasync def run_query(\n    prompt: str,\n    tools: list,\n hook_manager.set_transcript_path(str(session_history.path))\n\n    def _collect_hook_contexts(result: Any) -> List[str]:\n        contexts: List[str] = []\n        additional_context = getattr(result, "additional_context", None)\n        if additional_context:\n            contexts.append(str(additional_context))\n        return contexts\n\n    def _print_hook_system_message(result: Any, event: await load_mcp_servers_async(Path.cwd())\n        dynamic_tools = await load_dynamic_mcp_tools_async(Path.cwd())\n        if dynamic_tools:\n            tools = merge_tools_with_dynamic(tools, dynamic_tools)\n            if allowed_tools is not None:\n                tools = filter_tools_by_names(tools, allowed_tools)\n            query_context.tools = tools\n        mcp_instructions = List[str] = []\n        if skill_instructions:\n            additional_instructions.append(skill_instructions)\n        memory_instructions = build_memory_instructions()\n        if memory_instructions:\n            additional_instructions.append(memory_instructions)\n\n        with bind_pending_message_queue(query_context.pending_message_queue):\n            session_start_result = await hook_manager.run_session_start_async("startup")\n            _print_hook_system_message(session_start_result, "SessionStart")\n            session_hook_contexts = _collect_hook_contexts(session_start_result)\n            if session_hook_contexts:\n                additional_instructions.extend(session_hook_contexts)\n\n            prompt_hook_result = await hook_manager.run_user_prompt_submit_async(prompt)\n            if prompt_hook_result.should_block or not prompt_hook_result.should_continue:\n                reason = (\n                    prompt_hook_result.block_reason\n                    or prompt_hook_result.stop_reason\n                    or "Prompt blocked by hook."\n                )\n                console.print(f"[red]{escape(str(reason))}[/red]")\n                return\n            _print_hook_system_message(prompt_hook_result, "UserPromptSubmit")\n            prompt_hook_contexts = console.print(\n                            f"[yellow]Hook {escape(str(label))}[/yellow] {escape(str(text))}"\n                        )\n\n                if message.type == "assistant" and hasattr(message, "message"):\n                    # Collect assistant message text for final output\n                    if isinstance(message.message.content, str):\n                        final_response_parts.append(message.message.content)\n                    else:\n                        # Handle structured content\n                        for block in message.message.content:\n                            if isinstance(block, dict):\n                                if block.get("type") == "text":\n                                    final_response_parts.append(block["text"])\n                            else:\n                                if hasattr(block, "type") and block.type == "text":\n                                    final_response_parts.append(block.text or "")\n\n                # Skip progress messages entirely for -p mode\n\n                # Add message to history\n                messages.append(message)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n                session_history.append(message)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n            # Print final response as clean markdown (no panel, no decoration)\n            if final_response_parts:\n                final_text = "\\n".join(final_response_parts)\n                console.print(Markdown(final_text))\n\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            console.print("\\n[yellow]Interrupted by user[/yellow]")\n        except asyncio.CancelledError:\n            console.print("\\n[yellow]Operation cancelled[/yellow]")\n        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError, OSError, IOError, ConnectionError) as e:\n            console.print(f"[red]Error: {escape(str(e))}[/red]")\n            logger.warning(\n                "[cli] Unhandled error while running prompt: %s: %s",\n                type(e).__name__,\n                e,\n                extra={"session_id": session_id},\n            )\n            if verbose:\n                import traceback\n\n                console.print(traceback.format_exc(), markup=False)\n        logger.info(\n            "[cli] Prompt session completed",\n            extra={"session_id": session_id, "message_count":       "message_count": len(resume_messages) if resume_messages else 0,\n                "last_prompt": resumed_summary.last_prompt,\n                "log_file": str(log_file),\n            },\n        )\n    elif most_recent:\n        logger.info(\n            "[cli] Continuing session",\n            extra={\n                "session_id": session_id,\n                "message_count": complete\n    if not check_onboarding():\n        logger.info(\n            "[cli] Onboarding check failed or aborted; exiting.",\n            extra={"session_id": session_id},\n        )\n "Use only one of --init, --init-only, or --maintenance."\n        )\n\n    setup_trigger: Optional[str] = None\n    if setup_init or setup_init_only:\n        setup_trigger = "init"\n    elif setup_maintenance:\n        setup_trigger = "maintenance"\n\n    if setup_trigger:\n        hook_manager.set_project_dir(project_path)\n        hook_manager.set_session_id(session_id)\n    return\n\n    # Handle piped stdin input\n    # - With -p flag: Not applicable (prompt comes from -p argument)\n    # - Without -p: stdin becomes the initial query in interactive mode\n    initial_query: Optional[str] = None\n    if prompt is None and ctx.invoked_subcommand is None:\n        stdin_stream = click.get_text_stream("stdin")\n        try:\n            stdin_is_tty =         exc,\n                    extra={"session_id": session_id},\n                )\n            else:\n                trimmed = stdin_data.rstrip("\\n")\n                if trimmed.strip():\n                    initial_query = trimmed\n                    logger.info(\n                        "[cli] Received initial query from stdin",\n                        extra={\n "internvl2",\n}\n\n\nclass ModelProfile(BaseModel):\n    """Configuration for a specific AI model."""\n\n    provider: ProviderType\n    model: str\n    api_key: Optional[str] = None\n    # Anthropic supports either api_key or auth_token; api_key takes precedence when both are set.\n    auth_token: Optional[str] = None\n    api_base: Optional[str] = None\n    max_tokens: int = 4096\n    temperature: float = 0.7\n    # Total context window in tokens (if known). Falls back to heuristics when unset.\n    context_window: Optional[int] = None\n    # Tool handling for OpenAI-compatible providers. "native" uses tool_calls, "text" flattens tool\n    # interactions into plain text to support providers that reject tool roles.\n    openai_tool_mode: Literal["native", "text"] = "native"\n    # Optional override for thinking protocol handling (e.g., "deepseek", "openrouter",\n    # "qwen", "gemini_openai", "openai"). When unset, provider heuristics are used.\n    thinking_mode: Optional[str] = None\n    # Vision support flag. None = auto-detect based on model name, True/False = override.\n    supports_vision: Optional[bool] = None\n    # Pricing (USD per 1M tokens). Leave as 0 to skip cost calculation.\n    input_cost_per_million_tokens: float = 0.0\n    output_cost_per_million_tokens: float = 0.0\n\n\ndef model_supports_vision(model_profile: ModelProfile) -> bool:\n    """Detect whether a model supports vision/image input.\n\n    Args:\n        model_profile: The model profile to check\n\n    Returns:\n        True if the model supports vision capabilities, False otherwise\n    """\n    # If explicitly configured, use the config value\n    if model_profile.supports_vision is not None:\n        return model_profile.supports_vision\n\n    # Auto-detect based on model name\n    model_name = model_profile.model.lower()\n    return any(pattern in model_name for pattern in VISION_ENABLED_MODELS)\n\n\nclass ModelPointers(BaseModel):\n    """Pointers to different model profiles for different purposes."""\n\n    main: str = "default"\n    quick: str = "default"\n\n\nclass UserConfig(BaseModel):\n    """User configuration stored in ~/.ripperdoc.json"""\n\n    model_config = {"protected_namespaces": (), "populate_by_name": True}\n\n    # Model configuration\n    model_profiles: Dict[str, ModelProfile] = has_completed_onboarding: bool = False\n    last_onboarding_version: Optional[str] = None\n\n    # Statistics\n    num_startups: int = 0\n\n    @model_validator(mode="before")\n    @classmethod\n    def _migrate_safe_mode(cls, data: Any) -> Any:\n        """Translate legacy safe_mode to the new yolo_mode flag."""\n        if isinstance(data, dict) and "safe_mode" in data and "yolo_mode" not in data:\n            data = dict(data)\n            try:\n                data["yolo_mode"] = not bool(data.pop("safe_mode"))\n            except Exception:\n                data["yolo_mode"] = False\n        return data\n\n\nclass ProjectConfig(BaseModel):\n    """Project-specific configuration stored in .ripperdoc/config.json"""\n\n    # Tool permissions (project level - checked into git)\n Field(default_factory=list)\n\n\nclass ConfigManager:\n    """Manages user and project-specific configuration."""\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.global_config_path = Path.home() / ".ripperdoc.json"\n        self.current_project_path: Optional[Path] = None\n        self._global_config: Optional[UserConfig] = None\n        self._project_config: Optional[ProjectConfig] = None\n        self._project_local_config: Optional[ProjectLocalConfig] = None\n\n    def get_global_config(self) -> UserConfig:\n        """Load and return user configuration."""\n        if self._global_config is None:\n            if self.global_config_path.exists():\n                try:\n                    data = json.loads(self.global_config_path.read_text())\n                    self._global_config =               json.JSONDecodeError,\n                    OSError,\n                    IOError,\n                    UnicodeDecodeError,\n                    ValueError,\n                    TypeError,\n                ) as e:\n                    logger.warning(\n                        "Error loading user config: %s: %s",\n                        type(e).__name__,\n                        e,\n                        extra={"error": str(e)},\n                    )\n                    self._global_config = UserConfig()\n            else:\n                self._global_config = UserConfig()\n                logger.debug(\n                    "[config] User config not found; using defaults",\n                    extra={"path": str(self.global_config_path)},\n                )\n        return self._global_config\n\n    def save_global_config(self, config: UserConfig) -> None:\n        """Save user configuration."""\n        self._global_config = config\n project config when switching projects\n            if self.current_project_path != project_path:\n                self._project_config = None\n            self.current_project_path = project_path\n\n        if self.current_project_path is None:\n            return ProjectConfig()\n\n        config_path = self.current_project_path / ".ripperdoc" / "config.json"\n\n        if self._project_config is             "path": str(config_path),\n                        "project_path": str(self.current_project_path),\n                    },\n                )\n\n        return self._project_config\n\n config_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n\n        config_path = config_dir / "config.json"\n        self._project_config = config\n        config_path.write_text(config.model_dump_json(indent=2))\n save_project_local_config(\n        self, config: ProjectLocalConfig, project_path: Optional[Path] = None\n    ) -> None:\n        """Save project-local configuration."""\n        if project_path is not None:\n            self.current_project_path = project_path\n\n        if self.current_project_path is None:\n            return\n\n        config_dir = self.current_project_path / ".ripperdoc"\n        config_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n\n        config_path = config_dir / "config.local.json"\n        self._project_local_config = config\n        config_path.write_text(config.model_dump_json(indent=2))\n\n        # Ensure config.local.json is in .gitignore\n        self._ensure_gitignore_entry("config.local.json")\n\n        logger.debug(\n )\n\n    def _ensure_gitignore_entry(self, entry: str) -> bool:\n        """Ensure an entry exists in .ripperdoc/.gitignore. Returns True if added."""\n        if self.current_project_path is None:\n            return False\n\n        gitignore_path = self.current_project_path / ".ripperdoc" / ".gitignore"\n        try:\n            text = ""\n            if gitignore_path.exists():\n                text = gitignore_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")\n                existing_lines = text.splitlines()\n                if entry in existing_lines:\n                    return False\n            with gitignore_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:\n                if text and not text.endswith("\\n"):\n                    f.write("\\n")\n                f.write(f"{entry}\\n")\n            return True\n        except (OSError, IOError):\n            return False\n\n    def get_api_key(self, provider: ProviderType) -> Optional[str]:\n        """Get API key for a provider."""\n        # First check environment variables\n        env_candidates = api_key_env_candidates(provider)\n        provider_env = f"{provider.value.upper()}_API_KEY"\n        if provider_env not in env_candidates:\n            env_candidates.insert(0, provider_env)\n        for env_var in env_candidates:\n            if env_var in os.environ:\n                return os.environ[env_var]\n\n        # Then check user config\n        global_config = self.get_global_config()\n        for profile in global_config.model_profiles.values():\n            if profile.provider == provider and profile.api_key:\n                return profile.api_key\n\n        return None\n\n    def get_current_model_profile(self, pointer: str = "main") -> Optional[ModelProfile]:\n        """Get the current model profile for a given pointer."""\n        global_config = self.get_global_config()\n\n        # Get the profile name from the pointer\n        profile_name = getattr(global_config.model_pointers, pointer, "default")\n\n        # Return the profile\n        return global_config.model_profiles.get(profile_name)\n\n    def _fallback_profile_name(self, preferred: str = "default") -> str:\n        """Pick a valid profile name to use as a fallback for pointers."""\n        config = self.get_global_config()\n        if preferred in config.model_profiles:\n            return preferred\n        if config.model_profiles:\n            return next(iter(config.model_profiles.keys()))\n        return ""\n\n    def add_model_profile(\n        self,\n        name: str,\n        profile: ModelProfile,\n        overwrite: bool = False,\n        set_as_main: bool = False,\n    ) -> UserConfig:\n        """Add or replace a model profile and optionally set it as the main pointer."""\n        config = self.get_global_config()\n        if not overwrite and name in config.model_profiles:\n            raise ValueError(f"Model profile \'{name}\' already exists.")\n\n        config.model_profiles[name] = profile\n        current_main = getattr(config.model_pointers, "main", "")\n        if set_as_main or not current_main or current_main not in config.model_profiles:\n            config.model_pointers.main = name\n        self.save_global_config(config)\n        return config\n\n    def delete_model_profile(self, name: str) -> UserConfig:\n        """Delete a model profile and repair pointers that referenced it."""\n        config = self.get_global_config()\n        if name not in config.model_profiles:\n            raise KeyError(f"Model profile \'{name}\' does not exist.")\n\n        del config.model_profiles[name]\n\n        fallback = self._fallback_profile_name()\n        for pointer_field in ModelPointers.model_fields:\n            current = getattr(config.model_pointers, pointer_field, "")\n            if current == name:\n                setattr(config.model_pointers, pointer_field, fallback)\n\n        self.save_global_config(config)\n        return config\n\n    def set_model_pointer(self, pointer: str, profile_name: str) -> UserConfig:\n        """Point a logical model slot (e.g., main/quick) to a profile name."""\n        if pointer not in ModelPointers.model_fields:\n            raise ValueError(f"Unknown model pointer \'{pointer}\'.")\n\n        config = self.get_global_config()\n        if profile_name not in config.model_profiles:\n            raise ValueError(f"Model profile \'{profile_name}\' does not exist.")\n\n        setattr(config.model_pointers, pointer, profile_name)\n        self.save_global_config(config)\n        return config\n\n\n# Global instance\nconfig_manager = ConfigManager()\n\n\ndef get_global_config() -> UserConfig:\n    """Get user configuration."""\n    return configuration."""\n    config_manager.save_project_local_config(config, project_path)\n\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# RIPPERDOC_* 全局环境变量支持\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# 环境变量名称常量\nRIPPERDOC_BASE_URL = "RIPPERDOC_BASE_URL"\nRIPPERDOC_AUTH_TOKEN = "RIPPERDOC_AUTH_TOKEN"\nRIPPERDOC_MODEL = "RIPPERDOC_MODEL"\nRIPPERDOC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL = "RIPPERDOC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL"\nRIPPERDOC_API_KEY = "RIPPERDOC_API_KEY"\nRIPPERDOC_PROTOCOL = "RIPPERDOC_PROTOCOL"\n\n\ndef _infer_protocol_from_url_and_model(base_url: str, model_name: str = "") -> ProviderType:\n    """根据 BASE_URL 和模型名称推断协议类型.\n\n    Args:\n        base_url: API基础URL\n        model_name: 模型名称\n\n    Returns:\n        推断的 ProviderType\n    """\n    base_lower = base_url.lower()\n    model_lower = model_name.lower()\n\n    # 显式域名检测\n    if "anthropic.com" in base_lower:\n        return ProviderType.ANTHROPIC\n    if "generativelanguage.googleapis.com" in base_lower or "gemini" in model_lower:\n        return ProviderType.GEMINI\n\n    # URL 路径检测 - 检查路径中是否包含协议标识\n    if "/anthropic" in base_lower or base_lower.endswith("/anthropic"):\n        return ProviderType.ANTHROPIC\n    if "/v1/" in base_lower or "/v1" in base_lower:\n        # 大多数 /v1/ 路径是 OpenAI 兼容格式\n        return ProviderType.OPENAI_COMPATIBLE\n\n    # 模型名称前缀检测\n    if model_lower.startswith("claude-"):\n        return ProviderType.ANTHROPIC\n    if model_lower.startswith("gemini-"):\n        return ProviderType.GEMINI\n\n    # 默认使用 OpenAI 兼容协议\n    return ProviderType.OPENAI_COMPATIBLE\n\n\ndef _get_ripperdoc_env_overrides() -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    """获取所有 RIPPERDOC_* 环境变量的值.\n\n    Returns:\n        包含所有已设置环境变量的字典\n    """\n    overrides: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n    if base_url := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_BASE_URL):\n        overrides["base_url"] = base_url\n    if api_key := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_API_KEY):\n        overrides["api_key"] = api_key\n    if auth_token := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_AUTH_TOKEN):\n        overrides["auth_token"] = auth_token\n    if model := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_MODEL):\n        overrides["model"] = model\n    if small_fast_model := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL):\n        overrides["small_fast_model"] = small_fast_model\n    if protocol_str := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_PROTOCOL):\n        try:\n            overrides["protocol"] = ProviderType(protocol_str.lower())\n        except ValueError:\n            logger.warning(\n                "[config] Invalid RIPPERDOC_PROTOCOL value: %s (must be anthropic, openai_compatible, or gemini)",\n                protocol_str,\n            )\n    return overrides\n\n\ndef has_ripperdoc_env_overrides() -> bool:\n    """检查是否设置了任何 RIPPERDOC_* 环境变量."""\n    return bool(_get_ripperdoc_env_overrides())\n\n\ndef get_effective_model_profile(pointer: str = "main") -> Optional[ModelProfile]:\n    """获取模型配置，应用 RIPPERDOC_* 环境变量覆盖.\n\n    当设置了 RIPPERDOC_BASE_URL 环境变量时，完全在内存中创建 ModelProfile，\n    不依赖也不写入配置文件。这是获取模型配置的新入口点，替代 get_current_model_profile()。\n\n    Args:\n        pointer: 模型指针名称 ("main" 或 "quick")\n\n    Returns:\n        应用环境变量覆盖后的 ModelProfile，如果没有则返回 None\n    """\n    env_overrides = _get_ripperdoc_env_overrides()\n    base_url = env_overrides.get("base_url")\n\n    # 如果设置了 RIPPERDOC_BASE_URL，完全在内存中创建 profile\n    if base_url:\n        # 确定模型名称\n        if pointer == "quick":\n            model_name = env_overrides.get("small_fast_model") or env_overrides.get("model")\n        else:\n            model_name = env_overrides.get("model")\n\n        if not model_name:\n            model_name = "claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929"\n\n        # 确定协议类型\n        protocol = env_overrides.get("protocol")\n        if not protocol:\n            protocol = _infer_protocol_from_url_and_model(base_url, model_name)\n\n        # 在内存中创建新的 profile，不写入配置文件\n        return ModelProfile(\n            provider=protocol,\n            model=model_name,\n            api_base=base_url,\n            api_key=env_overrides.get("api_key"),\n            auth_token=env_overrides.get("auth_token"),\n        )\n\n    # 没有设置 RIPPERDOC_BASE_URL，返回配置文件中的 profile\n    return get_current_model_profile(pointer)\n\n\ndef get_ripperdoc_env_status() -> Dict[str, str]:\n    """获取 RIPPERDOC_* 环境变量状态信息用于诊断显示.\n\n    Returns:\n        字典，键为环境变量名称，值为格式化的显示字符串\n    """\n    status: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    if base_url := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_BASE_URL):\n        status["BASE_URL"] = base_url\n    if protocol := os.getenv(RIPPERDOC_PROTOCOL):\n        status["PROTOCOL"] =   lines = raw_text.splitlines()\n    if len(lines) >= 3 and lines[0].strip() == "---":\n        for idx in range(1, len(lines)):\n            if lines[idx].strip() == "---":\n SubagentStop for subagent-scoped hooks."""\n    if "Stop" not in hooks_data:\n        return hooks_data\n    converted = dict(hooks_data)\n    stop_matchers = converted.pop("Stop")\n    existing = converted.get("SubagentStop")\n    if existing is None:\n        converted["SubagentStop"] = stop_matchers\n    elif isinstance(existing, list) and isinstance(stop_matchers, list):\n _normalize_agent_hooks(raw_hooks: object) -> object:\n    if not isinstance(raw_hooks, dict):\n        return raw_hooks\n    if "hooks" in raw_hooks and isinstance(raw_hooks.get("hooks"), dict):\n _parse_agent_file(\n    path: Path, location: AgentLocation\n) -> Tuple[Optional[AgentDefinition], Optional[str]]:\n    """Parse a single agent file."""\n    try:\n        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")\n    except (OSError, IOError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:\n        logger.warning(\n            "Failed to read agent file: %s: %s",\n            type(exc).__name__,\n            exc,\n            extra={"error": str(exc), "path": str(path)},\n        )\n        return None, f"Failed to read agent file {path}: {exc}"\n\n    frontmatter, body = frontmatter.get("description")\n    if not isinstance(agent_name, str) or not agent_name.strip():\n        return None, \'Missing required "name" field in frontmatter\'\n    if not isinstance(description, str) or not description.strip():\n        return None, \'Missing required "description" field in frontmatter\'\n\n    tools = _normalize_tools(frontmatter.get("tools"))\n isinstance(color_value, str) else None\n    fork_context = parse_boolish(frontmatter.get("fork_context") or frontmatter.get("fork-context"))\n    hooks = parse_hooks_config(\n when_to_use=description.replace("\\\\n", "\\n").strip(),\n        tools=tools,\n        system_prompt=body.strip(),\n        location=location,\n        model=model,\n        color=color,\n Tuple[List[AgentDefinition], List[Tuple[Path, str]]]:\n    agents: List[AgentDefinition] = []\n    errors: List[Tuple[Path, str]] = []\n    if not path.exists():\n        return agents, errors\n\n summarize_agent(agent: AgentDefinition) -> str:\n    """Short human-readable summary."""\n    tool_label = "all tools" if "*" in agent.tools else ", ".join(agent.tools)\n    location = available_tools: Iterable[object], task_tool_name: str\n) -> Tuple[List[object], List[str]]:\n    """Map tool names from an agent to Tool instances, filtering out the task tool itself."""\n    tool_map: Dict[str, object] = {}\n    ordered_tools: List[object] = []\n    for tool in available_tools:\n        name = getattr(tool, "name", None)\n        if not name:\n            continue\n        if name == task_tool_name:\n            continue\n        tool_map[name] = tool\n        ordered_tools.append(tool)\n\n    if "*" in agent.tools:\n        return ordered_tools, []\n\n    resolved: List[object] = []\n    missing: List[str] = []\n    seen = set()\n    for tool_name in agent.tools:\n        if tool_name in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(tool_name)\n        tool = tool_map.get(tool_name)\n        if tool:\n            resolved.append(tool)\n        else:\n            missing.append(tool_name)\n    return resolved, missing\n\n\ndef save_agent_definition(\n Complete tasks fully; do not stop mid-task or claim context limits.",\n        ]\n    )\n    doing_tasks_section = "\\n".join(doing_tasks_lines)\n\n    tool_usage_lines = [\n        "# Tool usage policy",\n        \'- You have the capability to call multiple tools in a single response. When multiple independent pieces of information are requested, batch your tool calls together for optimal performance. When making multiple bash tool calls, you MUST send a single message with multiple tools calls to run the calls in parallel. For example, if you need to run "git status" and "git diff", send a single message with two tool calls to run the calls in parallel.\',\n        "- If the user asks to run tools in parallel and there are no dependencies, include multiple tool calls in a single message; sequence dependent calls instead of guessing values.",\n        f"- Use specialized tools instead of bash when possible: use {view_tool_name} for reading files, {file_edit_tool_name} for editing, and {file_write_tool_name} for creating files. Global hooks (apply to all projects)\n- `.ripperdoc/hooks.json` - Project hooks (checked into git)\n- `.ripperdoc/hooks.local.json` - Local hooks (git-ignored)\n\n### Configuration Format\n\n```json\n{\n  "hooks": {\n    "EventName": [\n      {\n        "matcher": "ToolPattern",\n        "hooks": [\n          {\n            "type": "command",\n            "command": "your-command-here",\n            "timeout": 60\n          }\n        ]\n      }\n    ]\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Matcher Patterns\n\nFor `PreToolUse` and `PostToolUse` events:\n\n- Exact match: `"Bash"`, `"Write"`, `"Edit"`\n- Regex pattern: `"Edit|Write"`, `"mcp__.*__write.*"`\n- Match all: `"*"` or omit/empty\n\n### Environment Variables\n\nHooks receive these environment variables:\n- `RIPPERDOC_PROJECT_DIR`: Path to the project directory\n\n### Input/Output\n\nHooks receive JSON input via stdin containing event-specific data.\n\nHooks can output:\n- Plain text (added as context)\n- JSON with decision control:\n\n```json\n{\n  "decision": "allow|deny|ask|block",\n  "reason": "Explanation shown to user/model",\n  "suppressOutput": false,\n  "additionalContext": "Context to add to conversation"\n}\n```\n\n### Exit Codes\n\n- `0`: Success, continue normally\n- `2`: Block/deny the operation\n- Other: Error occurred\n\n## Examples\n\n### log_bash.py\nLogs all Bash commands to a file.\n\n### check_write.py\nPrevents writing to sensitive files.\n\n### check_prompt.py\nFilters sensitive information from prompts.\n\n### session_start.py / session_end.py\nTrack session lifecycle events.\n\n### log_notification.py\nSend desktop notifications.\n\n## Quick Start\n\n1. Let me create a detailed analysis summary:\n\n# QuantmewRipperdoc 项目详细分析报告\n\n## 项目概述\n\n**Ripperdoc** 是一个开源、可扩展的 AI 编程代理（AI Coding Agent），在终端中运行。它类似于 Claude Code、Aider、Goose 等工具，通过自然语言对话帮助开发者完成编程任务。\n\n- **版本**: 0.3.3\n- **许可证**: Apache 2.0\n- **Python 要求**: >= 3.10\n- **仓库地址**: https://github.com/quantmew/ripperdoc\n\n## 核心功能特性\n\n### 1. **AI 智能助手**\n- 使用 AI 模型理解和响应编码请求\n- 支持多模型：Anthropic Claude、OpenAI 兼容模型、Google Gemini\n- 自动检测和配置模型能力（如视觉支持）\n\n### 2. **丰富的工具系统**（20+ 内置工具）\n- **文件操作**: Read、Write、Edit、MultiEdit、LS、Glob、Grep\n- **命令执行**: Bash（支持后台运行）、KillBash\n- **代码编辑**: NotebookEdit（Jupyter 支持）\n- **任务管理**: TodoRead/Write\n- **代理系统**: Task（子代理委托）、Skill（技能加载）\n- **MCP 集成**: ListMcpServers、ListMcpResources、ReadMcpResource\n- **LSP 支持**: 语言服务器协议集成\n- **工具搜索**: ToolSearch（延迟加载工具发现）\n\n### 3. **子代理系统**\n内置 4 种专业代理：\n- **general-purpose**: 通用多步骤编码任务\n- **code-reviewer**: 代码审查（只读）\n- **explore**: 快速代码库探索（只读）\n- **plan**: 软件架构设计和实现计划（只读）\n\n### 4. **Agent Skills 系统**\n通过 SKILL.md 文件扩展能力，支持：\n- 个人技能：`~/.ripperdoc/skills/<skill-name>/SKILL.md`\n- 项目技能：`.ripperdoc/skills/<skill-name>/SKILL.md`\n- YAML frontmatter 配置（allowed-tools、model、max-thinking-tokens 等）\n- 内置技能：PDF、PowerPoint、Excel 处理\n\n### 5. **Hooks 系统**\n在生命周期事件执行自定义脚本：\n- **PreToolUse**: 工具调用前（可阻止执行）\n- **PostToolUse**: 工具完成后\n- **UserPromptSubmit**: 用户提交提示时\n- **SessionStart/End**: 会话开始/结束\n- **PermissionRequest**: 权限请求时\n- **SubagentStop**: 子代理完成时\n- 支持 JSON 配置和自定义 Python 脚本\n\n### 6. **权限系统**\n- **安全模式默认开启**（yolo_mode 关闭时）\n- 三级权限规则：allow、ask、deny\n- 工作目录限制\n- 命令验证和沙箱支持\n- 支持全局、项目、本地三层配置\n\n### 7. **会话管理**\n- 持久化会话历史\n- 会话继续（-c）和恢复（-r）\n- 会话分叉和分支\n- 自动对话压缩（context length 管理）\n- 会话统计和热力图\n\n### 8. **MCP（Model Context Protocol）支持**\n- 动态加载 MCP 服务器工具\n- 资源读取和列表查询\n- 配置文件：`.ripperdoc/mcp.json`\n\n### 9. **自定义命令系统**\n- 斜杠命令（如 `/help`、`/todo`、`/skills`）\n- 文件定义（frontmatter + bash 命令）\n- 参数替换支持\n- 模糊匹配和建议\n\n### 10. **Python SDK（无头模式）**\n- 子进程架构（与主进程隔离）\n- Claude Agent SDK 协议兼容\n- 支持一次性查询和会话模式\n- stdin/stdout JSON 流式协议\n\n## 技术架构\n\n### 目录结构\n```\nripperdoc/\n├── cli/                    # CLI 界面\n│   ├── cli.py             # 主入口点\n│   └── ui/                # Rich UI 组件\n├── core/                   # 核心功能\n│   ├── agents.py          # 子代理定义\n│   ├── config.py          # 配置管理\n│   ├── permissions.py     # 权限系统\n│   ├── skills.py          # Skills 加载\n│   ├── system_prompt.py   # 系统提示构建\n│   ├── tool.py            # 工具基类\n│   ├── providers/         # LLM 提供商\n│   └── hooks/             # Hooks 系统\n├── tools/                  # 工具实现\n│   ├── bash_tool.py\n│   ├── file_*.py\n│   └── ...\n├── utils/                  # 工具函数\n└── protocol/               # SDK 协议\n```\n\n### 核心模块说明\n\n#### 1. **配置管理**\n- 全局配置：`~/.ripperdoc.json`\n- 项目配置：`.ripperdoc/config.json`\n- 本地配置：`.ripperdoc/config.local.json`（git-ignored）\n- 环境变量支持：`RIPPERDOC_*` 前缀\n\n#### 2. **LLM 提供商**\n支持三种协议：\n- **anthropic**: Claude 模型\n- **openai_compatible**: OpenAI、DeepSeek、Mistral、Kimi、Qwen、GLM 等\n- **gemini**: Google Gemini 模型\n\n特性：\n- 自动协议检测\n- 视觉能力检测\n- Thinking 模式支持\n- 令牌和成本跟踪\n\n#### 3. **工具系统**\n所有工具继承自 `Tool[TInput, TOutput]` 基类：\n- `name`: 工具名称\n- `description()`: AI 描述\n- `input_schema`: Pydantic 输入模型\n- `call()`: 异步执行\n- `render_result_for_assistant()`: 结果渲染\n- `needs_permissions()`: 权限检查\n\n#### 4. **查询循环**\n```\n用户输入 → 权限检查 → 工具执行 → LLM 调用 → 响应渲染\n                ↓\n            Hooks 拦截\n```\n\n#### 5. **Hooks 架构**\n- 事件驱动\n- JSON 配置解析\n- Python 脚本执行\n- 决策控制\n- 上下文注入\n\n#### 6. **权限系统**\n```\n规则优先级: deny > ask > allow\n配置层级: 全局 > 项目 > 本地 > 会话\n```\n\n## 依赖配置（pyproject.toml）\n\n### 核心依赖\n```toml\ndependencies = [\n    "anthropic>=0.39.0",      # Claude API\n   "prompt-toolkit>=3.0.52", # 交互式输入\n    "mcp[cli]>=1.25.0",       # MCP 协议\n    "google-genai>=0.3.0",    # Gemini API\n    "tiktoken>=0.7.0",        # 令牌计数\n]\n```\n\n### 开发依赖\n```toml\ndev = [\n    "pytest>=7.0.0",          # 测试框架\n    "pytest-asyncio>=0.21.0", # 异步测试\n    "mypy>=1.0.0",            # 类型检查\n    "black>=23.0.0",          # 代码格式化\n    "ruff>=0.1.0",            # Linter\n]\n```\n\n## CLI 命令\n\n### 交互模式\n```bash\nripperdoc                    # 启动交互式会话\nrd                           # 短别名\nripperdoc -c                 # 继续上次会话\nripperdoc -r <session-id>    # 恢复特定会话\n```\n\n### 选项\n- `--yolo`: 跳过权限提示\n- `--model <name>`: 指定模型\n- `--tools <list>`: 过滤工具\n- `--no-mcp`: 禁用 MCP\n- `--verbose`: 详细日志\n- `--system-prompt`: 自定义系统提示\n- `--prompt <text>` / `-p`: 直接执行提示\n\n### 内置斜杠命令\n- `/help`: 帮助信息\n- `/todo`: 任务管理\n- `/tasks`: 任务列表\n- `/tools`: 工具列表\n- `/skills`: Skills 列表\n- `/agents`: 代理管理\n- `/models`: 模型管理\n- `/clear`: 清屏\n- `/exit`: 退出\n- `/fork`: 分叉会话\n- `/doctor`: 系统诊断\n\n## 使用场景\n\n### 1. **代码分析**\n```\n> 解释这个函数的作用\n> 查找所有 parse_config 函数的引用\n> 分析这个项目的架构\n```\n\n### 2. **文件操作**\n```\n> 读取 main.py 并建议改进\n> 创建 UserProfile.tsx 组件\n> 批量更新导入语句\n```\n\n### 3. **代码生成**\n```\n> 创建 REST API 客户端\n> 为 auth 模块生成单元测试\n> 添加错误处理到数据库连接\n```\n\n### 4. **项目导航**\n```\n> 显示所有 Python 文件\n> 找到用户认证逻辑\n> 列出所有 API 端点\n```\n\n### 5. **Git 操作**\n```\n> 创建提交并推送\n> 创建 Pull Request\n> 查看提交历史\n```\n\n### 6. **专业任务**\n```\n> 使用 explore 代理快速搜索代码\n> 使用 plan 代理设计实现方案\n> 使用 code-reviewer 代理审查代码\n> 加载 pdf skill 处理 PDF 文件\n```\n\n## 内置 Skills 示例\n\n### PDF Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the project. Let me create a detailed analysis summary:\n\n# QuantmewRipperdoc 项目详细分析报告\n\n## 项目概述\n\n**Ripperdoc** 是一个开源、可扩展的 AI 编程代理（AI Coding Agent），在终端中运行。它类似于 Claude Code、Aider、Goose 等工具，通过自然语言对话帮助开发者完成编程任务。\n\n- **版本**: 0.3.3\n- **许可证**: Apache 2.0\n- **Python 要求**: >= 3.10\n- **仓库地址**: https://github.com/quantmew/ripperdoc\n\n## 核心功能特性\n\n### 1. **AI 智能助手**\n- 使用 AI 模型理解和响应编码请求\n- 支持多模型：Anthropic Claude、OpenAI 兼容模型、Google Gemini\n- 自动检测和配置模型能力（如视觉支持）\n\n### 2. **丰富的工具系统**（20+ 内置工具）\n- **文件操作**: Read、Write、Edit、MultiEdit、LS、Glob、Grep\n- **命令执行**: Bash（支持后台运行）、KillBash\n- **代码编辑**: NotebookEdit（Jupyter 支持）\n- **任务管理**: TodoRead/Write\n- **代理系统**: Task（子代理委托）、Skill（技能加载）\n- **MCP 集成**: ListMcpServers、ListMcpResources、ReadMcpResource\n- **LSP 支持**: 语言服务器协议集成\n- **工具搜索**: ToolSearch（延迟加载工具发现）\n\n### 3. **子代理系统**\n内置 4 种专业代理：\n- **general-purpose**: 通用多步骤编码任务\n- **code-reviewer**: 代码审查（只读）\n- **explore**: 快速代码库探索（只读）\n- **plan**: 软件架构设计和实现计划（只读）\n\n### 4. **Agent Skills 系统**\n通过 SKILL.md 文件扩展能力，支持：\n- 个人技能：`~/.ripperdoc/skills/<skill-name>/SKILL.md`\n- 项目技能：`.ripperdoc/skills/<skill-name>/SKILL.md`\n- YAML frontmatter 配置（allowed-tools、model、max-thinking-tokens 等）\n- 内置技能：PDF、PowerPoint、Excel 处理\n\n### 5. **Hooks 系统**\n在生命周期事件执行自定义脚本：\n- **PreToolUse**: 工具调用前（可阻止执行）\n- **PostToolUse**: 工具完成后\n- **UserPromptSubmit**: 用户提交提示时\n- **SessionStart/End**: 会话开始/结束\n- **PermissionRequest**: 权限请求时\n- **SubagentStop**: 子代理完成时\n- 支持 JSON 配置和自定义 Python 脚本\n\n### 6. **权限系统**\n- **安全模式默认开启**（yolo_mode 关闭时）\n- 三级权限规则：allow、ask、deny\n- 工作目录限制\n- 命令验证和沙箱支持\n- 支持全局、项目、本地三层配置\n\n### 7. **会话管理**\n- 持久化会话历史\n- 会话继续（-c）和恢复（-r）\n- 会话分叉和分支\n- 自动对话压缩（context length 管理）\n- 会话统计和热力图\n\n### 8. **MCP（Model Context Protocol）支持**\n- 动态加载 MCP 服务器工具\n- 资源读取和列表查询\n- 配置文件：`.ripperdoc/mcp.json`\n\n### 9. **自定义命令系统**\n- 斜杠命令（如 `/help`、`/todo`、`/skills`）\n- 文件定义（frontmatter + bash 命令）\n- 参数替换支持\n- 模糊匹配和建议\n\n### 10. **Python SDK（无头模式）**\n- 子进程架构（与主进程隔离）\n- Claude Agent SDK 协议兼容\n- 支持一次性查询和会话模式\n- stdin/stdout JSON 流式协议\n\n## 技术架构\n\n### 目录结构\n```\nripperdoc/\n├── cli/                    # CLI 界面\n│   ├── cli.py             # 主入口点\n│   └── ui/                # Rich UI 组件\n├── core/                   # 核心功能\n│   ├── agents.py          # 子代理定义\n│   ├── config.py          # 配置管理\n│   ├── permissions.py     # 权限系统\n│   ├── skills.py          # Skills 加载\n│   ├── system_prompt.py   # 系统提示构建\n│   ├── tool.py            # 工具基类\n│   ├── providers/         # LLM 提供商\n│   └── hooks/             # Hooks 系统\n├── tools/                  # 工具实现\n│   ├── bash_tool.py\n│   ├── file_*.py\n│   └── ...\n├── utils/                  # 工具函数\n└── protocol/               # SDK 协议\n```\n\n### 核心模块说明\n\n#### 1. **配置管理**\n- 全局配置：`~/.ripperdoc.json`\n- 项目配置：`.ripperdoc/config.json`\n- 本地配置：`.ripperdoc/config.local.json`（git-ignored）\n- 环境变量支持：`RIPPERDOC_*` 前缀\n\n#### 2. **LLM 提供商**\n支持三种协议：\n- **anthropic**: Claude 模型\n- **openai_compatible**: OpenAI、DeepSeek、Mistral、Kimi、Qwen、GLM 等\n- **gemini**: Google Gemini 模型\n\n特性：\n- 自动协议检测\n- 视觉能力检测\n- Thinking 模式支持\n- 令牌和成本跟踪\n\n#### 3. **工具系统**\n所有工具继承自 `Tool[TInput, TOutput]` 基类：\n- `name`: 工具名称\n- `description()`: AI 描述\n- `input_schema`: Pydantic 输入模型\n- `call()`: 异步执行\n- `render_result_for_assistant()`: 结果渲染\n- `needs_permissions()`: 权限检查\n\n#### 4. **查询循环**\n```\n用户输入 → 权限检查 → 工具执行 → LLM 调用 → 响应渲染\n                ↓\n            Hooks 拦截\n```\n\n#### 5. **Hooks 架构**\n- 事件驱动\n- JSON 配置解析\n- Python 脚本执行\n- 决策控制\n- 上下文注入\n\n#### 6. **权限系统**\n```\n规则优先级: deny > ask > allow\n配置层级: 全局 > 项目 > 本地 > 会话\n```\n\n## 依赖配置（pyproject.toml）\n\n### 核心依赖\n```toml\ndependencies = [\n    "anthropic>=0.39.0",      # Claude API\n AI 智能助手\n- 支持多种 LLM 提供商：Anthropic Claude、OpenAI 兼容、Google Gemini\n- 自动检测模型能力（如视觉支持）\n- 流式响应与思考模式（thinking）\n\n### 2. 丰富的工具系统（20+ 内置工具）\n- **文件操作**：Read、Write、Edit、MultiEdit、LS、Glob、Grep\n- **命令执行**：Bash（支持后台运行）、KillBash\n- **代码编辑**：NotebookEdit（Jupyter 支持）\n- **任务管理**：TodoRead/Write\n- **子代理系统**：Task（委托子代理）、Skill（技能加载）\n- **MCP 集成**：动态加载 MCP 服务器工具\n- **LSP 支持**：语言服务器协议集成\n- **工具搜索**：ToolSearch（延迟工具发现）\n\n### 3. 子代理系统（4 种专业代理）\n| 子代理 | 用途 | 工具权限 |\n|--------|------|----------|\n| **general-purpose** | 通用多步骤编码任务 | 全部工具 |\n| **code-reviewer** | 代码审查 | 只读 |\n| **explore** | 快速代码库探索 | 只读 |\n| **plan** | 软件架构设计和实现计划 | 只读 |\n\n### 4. Agent Skills 系统\n通过 SKILL.md 文件扩展能力：\n- **个人技能**：`~/.ripperdoc/skills/<skill-name>/SKILL.md`\n- **项目技能**：`.ripperdoc/skills/<skill-name>/SKILL.md`\n- YAML frontmatter 配置（allowed-tools、model、max-thinking-tokens 等）\n- 内置技能：PDF、PowerPoint、Excel 处理\n\n### 5. Hooks 系统\n在生命周期事件执行自定义脚本：\n- PreToolUse、PostToolUse\n- UserPromptSubmit\n- SessionStart/End\n- PermissionRequest\n- SubagentStop\n- 支持 JSON 配置和 Python 脚本\n\n### 6. 权限系统\n- **安全模式默认开启**（yolo_mode 关闭时）\n- 三级权限规则：allow、ask、deny\n- 工作目录限制\n- 命令验证和沙箱支持\n- 三层配置：全局、项目、本地\n\n### 7. 会话管理\n- 持久化会话历史\n- 会话继续（`-c`）和恢复（`-r`）\n- 会话分叉和分支\n- 自动对话压缩（context length 管理）\n- 会话统计和热力图\n\n### 8. MCP（Model Context Protocol）支持\n- 动态加载 MCP 服务器工具\n- 资源读取和列表查询\n- 配置文件：`.ripperdoc/mcp.json`\n\n### 9. 自定义命令系统\n- 斜杠命令（如 `/help`、`/todo`、`/skills`）\n- 文件定义（frontmatter + bash 命令）\n- 参数替换支持\n- 模糊匹配和建议\n\n### 10.
